AB
1
These prompts are optimized for the free version of Nano Banana, which you can use in Google AI Studio and Gemini.
2
How to Use These Super Prompts

1.- Choose a super prompt and paste it into a new Nano Banana. Instantly, you’ll receive a complete set of text-only outputs: the book title, subtitle, description, keywords, the cover prompt, and all the illustration prompts needed to create every page of your adult cozy & cute coloring book.

2.- Generate the cover illustration by copying the cover prompt (provided in the output) and pasting it into a fresh image-generation message within the same chat. You’ll receive a high-quality, ready-to-use cover image for your book.

3.- Create the interior pages by taking each coloring-page prompt (provided in the output) and generating the illustrations one by one in the same chat. Tip: For maximum character and style consistency, continue editing the same image-generation message you used for the very first page—replace the text with the next page prompt each time until all pages are finished.

4.- Assemble your full coloring book using a design tool such as Canva.com. Import your cover and all completed coloring pages, arrange them in the order you prefer, and export the final book. You’ll now have a complete, professionally styled cozy adult coloring book ready to publish on Etsy, Amazon KDP, Gumroad, or your own online shop.
3
If you're not satisfied with an image, simply create it again by clicking "Rerun this turn".
4
Update: These prompts also work for ChatGPT, but since they are not optimized for it, you may occasionally need to delete the 'text only' instructions in the super prompt.
5
Here’s an example of a simple cute & cozy coloring book for adults. According to the Kindle Best Seller Calculator, the estimated royalty for this book is around $64,233 per month: https://www.amazon.com/Cozy-Friends-Featuring-Characters-Relaxation/dp/B0D4YLQRMP/ref=sr_1_6

Super PromptCategory
7
Create an adult coloring book featuring a fluffy cat who runs a cozy cat café filled with warm lighting, oversized teacups, and classic wooden shelves, occasionally interacting with a sleepy corgi customer or a curious squirrel peeking through the window. Generate a complete adult-friendly coloring book package based on the prompt above, but do not generate any images; output text only. All prompts must be written specifically for image generation and must later create final illustrations, but for now only text should be produced. Every coloring page prompt must follow a refined “cute cozy medium-detail” adult aesthetic with complete, layered scenes that never feel empty, but all details must remain clean, bold, and easy to color. Absolutely avoid dense clusters of small shapes; all vegetation, plants, flowers, leaves, and garden elements must be drawn using large, simple, stylized shapes with wide line spacing, zero micro-veins, zero fine-line textures, and no hyper-detailed botanical patterns. Any wallpapers, wall patterns, textiles, or background motifs must use big, spaced-out shapes to avoid visual noise. Scenes must include fully developed cozy environments with foreground, midground, and background elements such as shelves, lamps, cushions, windows, curtains, tables, framed art, rugs, cozy furniture, and gentle outdoor scenery, while maintaining readability and avoiding clutter. Characters must maintain consistent kawaii proportions and expressive poses; the main character may appear alone or together with one or more secondary characters in some scenes, but not in every scene, and all character interactions must remain warm, cute, and cohesive with the overall cozy world. Immediately output a catchy SEO-friendly title, a descriptive subtitle, a 3–5 sentence commercial description for Amazon KDP/Etsy emphasizing cozy charm and adult relaxation, and seven SEO keywords. Then output one full-color cover illustration prompt—text only—that includes the title and subtitle, displays a warm premium cozy aesthetic with multiple large, readable props and clear decorative elements, and states “Coloring Book for Adults.” After that, provide 20–30 interior illustration prompts, written only as text, each describing a finished black-and-white coloring page with medium detail, large clear decorative shapes, simplified vegetation, spaced-out background motifs, cozy, fully developed scenes, and an optional mix of solo scenes and scenes featuring the main character together with secondary characters. Maintain stylistic cohesion across all prompts and do not generate images at this time.Cozy Cat Café

Some scenes include a gentle fox or a chubby raccoon friend sharing hot cocoa or reading by the fire. Generate a complete adult-friendly coloring book package based on the prompt above, but do not generate any images; output text only. All prompts must be written specifically for image generation and must later create final illustrations, but for now only text should be produced. Every coloring page prompt must follow a refined “cute cozy medium-detail” adult aesthetic with complete, layered scenes that never feel empty, but all details must remain clean, bold, and easy to color. Absolutely avoid dense clusters of small shapes; all vegetation, plants, flowers, leaves, and garden elements must be drawn using large, simple, stylized shapes with wide line spacing, zero micro-veins, zero fine-line textures, and no hyper-detailed botanical patterns. Any wallpapers, wall patterns, textiles, or background motifs must use big, spaced-out shapes to avoid visual noise. Scenes must include fully developed cozy environments with foreground, midground, and background elements such as shelves, lamps, cushions, windows, curtains, tables, framed art, rugs, cozy furniture, and gentle outdoor scenery, while maintaining readability and avoiding clutter. Characters must maintain consistent kawaii proportions and expressive poses; the main character may appear alone or together with one or more secondary characters in some scenes, but not in every scene, and all character interactions must remain warm, cute, and cohesive with the overall cozy world. Immediately output a catchy SEO-friendly title, a descriptive subtitle, a 3–5 sentence commercial description for Amazon KDP/Etsy emphasizing cozy charm and adult relaxation, and seven SEO keywords. Then output one full-color cover illustration prompt—text only—that includes the title and subtitle, displays a warm premium cozy aesthetic with multiple large, readable props and clear decorative elements, and states “Coloring Book for Adults.” After that, provide 20–30 interior illustration prompts, written only as text, each describing a finished black-and-white coloring page with medium detail, large clear decorative shapes, simplified vegetation, spaced-out background motifs, cozy, fully developed scenes, and an optional mix of solo scenes and scenes featuring the main character together with secondary characters. Maintain stylistic cohesion across all prompts and do not generate images at this time.Cozy Fireplace Scenes
